# Lazy root observation — complete metrics

Raw captures referenced in this note are local experiment artifacts and are excluded from Git.
Published chart data is retained under `apps/docs/src/data`.

Cells are p50 / p75 / p95 / p99. Exact-before cells show eligible control-normalized history followed by the raw historical value in parentheses. Deterministic metrics retain raw history. Normalization controls are React, SvelteKit, and Nuxt; TanStack Start is excluded because it shares React’s renderer.
